Compute unclamped output\n        output = P + I + D\n\n        # Apply output limits\n        clamped_output = np.clip(output, self.min_output, self.max_output)\n\n        # Anti-windup: back-calculate integral\n        if self.Ki != 0 and output != clamped_output:\n            self.integral -= (output - clamped_output) / self.Ki\n\n        return clamped_output\n\n    def reset(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset controller state.\"\"\"\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n\ndef run_calibration(sim, heater_power=50.0, duration=30.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run open-loop calibration test.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        heater_power: Constant heater power for test (%)\n        duration: Test duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with calibration log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running calibration test: {heater_power}% power for {duration}s\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    initial_temp = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial temperature: {initial_temp:.2f}C\")\n\n    # Collect data with heater off first (initial condition)\n    data = []\n    data.append({\n        \"time\": 0.0,\n        \"temperature\": initial_temp,\n        \"heater_power\": 0.0\n    })\n\n    # Run open-loop test\n    results = sim.run_open_loop(heater_power, duration)\n\n    # Add results to data\n    for result in results:\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": result[\"time\"],\n            \"temperature\": result[\"temperature\"],\n            \"heater_power\": result[\"heater_power\"]\n        })\n\n    print(f\"Calibration complete: {len(data)} data points collected\")\n\n    calibration_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"calibration\",\n        \"heater_power_test\": heater_power,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    return calibration_log\n\n\ndef estimate_parameters(calibration_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Estimate system parameters from calibration data.\n\n    Args:\n        calibration_log: Calibration log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with estimated parameters\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Estimating system parameters from calibration data...\")\n\n    data = calibration_log[\"data\"]\n    heater_power = calibration_log[\"heater_power_test\"]\n\n    # Extract time and temperature arrays (skip first point where heater is off)\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data[1:]])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data[1:]])\n\n    # Adjust time to start from 0\n    times = times - times[0]\n\n    # Initial temperature (before step)\n    T0 = data[0][\"temperature\"]\n\n    # Define first-order step response model\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        \"\"\"First-order step response: T(t) = T0 + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\"\"\"\n        return T0 + K * heater_power * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Initial parameter guesses\n    K_guess = 0.1  # Process gain\n    tau_guess = 30.0  # Time constant\n\n    try:\n        # Fit the model\n        params, covariance = curve_fit(\n            first_order_response,\n            times,\n            temps,\n            p0=[K_guess, tau_guess],\n            bounds=([0.01, 5.0], [0.5, 100.0]),\n            maxfev=5000\n        )\n\n        K_est, tau_est = params\n\n        # Calculate fit quality\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot)\n\n        # Fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.4f}, tau = {tau_est:.2f}s\")\n        print(f\"R² = {r_squared:.4f}, RMSE = {fitting_error:.4f}\")\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        return estimated_params\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        raise\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=1.5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PID controller gains using lambda tuning.\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: Tuning parameter (multiplier of tau)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Calculating controller gains...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # Lambda tuning (IMC-based)\n    lambda_val = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda_val)\n    Ki = 1.0 / lambda_val\n    Kd = 0.0  # Not needed for thermal systems\n\n    print(f\"Controller gains: Kp={Kp:.4f}, Ki={Ki:.4f}, Kd={Kd:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda = {lambda_val:.2f}s\")\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_val, 4)\n    }\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint, duration=150.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Reset simulator to initial conditions\n    initial_temp = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Reset to initial temperature: {initial_temp:.2f}C\")\n\n    # Initialize PID controller\n    dt = sim.get_dt()\n    controller = PIDController(\n        Kp=tuned_gains[\"Kp\"],\n        Ki=tuned_gains[\"Ki\"],\n        Kd=tuned_gains[\"Kd\"],\n        dt=dt,\n        min_output=0.0,\n        max_output=100.0\n    )\n\n    # Collect control data\n    data = []\n    steps = int(duration / dt)\n\n    # Get initial measurement\n    current_temp = initial_temp\n\n    for i in range(steps):\n        # Compute control output\n        error = setpoint - current_temp\n        heater_power = controller.compute(setpoint, current_temp)\n\n        # Apply control and step simulator\n        result = sim.step(heater_power)\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = result[\"time\"]\n\n        # Log data\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(heater_power, 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })\n\n        # Progress update\n        if (i + 1) % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={current_time:.1f}s: T={current_temp:.2f}C, P={heater_power:.1f}%, e={error:.2f}C\")\n\n    print(f\"Closed-loop control complete: {len(data)} data points\")\n\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    return control_log\n\n\ndef calculate_metrics(control_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate performance metrics from control log.\n\n    Args:\n        control_log: Control log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating performance metrics...\")\n\n    data = control_log[\"data\"]\n    setpoint = control_log[\"setpoint\"]\n\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n    errors = np.array([d[\"error\"] for d in data])\n\n    initial_temp = temps[0]\n    initial_time = times[0]\n\n    # 1. Rise time (time to reach 90% of setpoint from initial)\n    target_90 = initial_temp + 0.9 * (setpoint - initial_temp)\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= target_90)[0]\n    if len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - initial_time\n    else:\n        rise_time = None\n\n    # 2. Overshoot (percentage above setpoint)\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / setpoint\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # 3. Settling time (time to stay within ±2% of setpoint)\n    tolerance = 0.02\n    tolerance_value = abs(setpoint * tolerance)\n    lower_bound = setpoint - tolerance_value\n    upper_bound = setpoint + tolerance_value\n\n    in_band = (temps >= lower_bound) & (temps <= upper_bound)\n\n    # Find last time it was outside the band\n    outside_indices = np.where(~in_band)[0]\n    if len(outside_indices) > 0:\n        settling_idx = outside_indices[-1] + 1\n        if settling_idx < len(times):\n            settling_time = times[settling_idx] - initial_time\n        else:\n            settling_time = None\n    else:\n        # Always in band\n        settling_time = 0.0\n\n    # 4. Steady-state error (average of last 20% of data)\n    last_n = max(20, len(temps) // 5)\n    final_temps = temps[-last_n:]\n    avg_final = np.mean(final_temps)\n    steady_state_error = abs(setpoint - avg_final)\n\n    # 5. Maximum temperature\n    max_temp_reached = np.max(temps)\n\n    print(f\"  Rise time: {rise_time:.2f}s\" if rise_time else \"  Rise time: N/A\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {settling_time:.2f}s\" if settling_time else \"  Settling time: N/A\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f}C\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature: {max_temp_reached:.2f}C\")\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4) if rise_time is not None else None,\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4) if settling_time is not None else None,\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp_reached, 4)\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Temperature Controller Implementation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = HVACSimulator(\"/root/room_config.json\")\n\n    # Step 1: Run calibration test\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"STEP 1: Calibration Test\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    calibration_log = run_calibration(sim, heater_power=50.0, duration=30.0)\n\n    # Save calibration log\n    with open(\"/root/calibration_log.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(calibration_log, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"Saved: calibration_log.json\")\n\n    # Step 2: Estimate parameters\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"STEP 2: Parameter Estimation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    estimated_params = estimate_parameters(calibration_log)\n\n    # Save estimated parameters\n    with open(\"/root/estimated_params.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(estimated_params, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"Saved: estimated_params.json\")\n\n    # Step 3: Calculate controller gains\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"STEP 3: Controller Gain Tuning\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    tuned_gains = calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=1.5)\n\n    # Save tuned gains\n    with open(\"/root/tuned_gains.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(tuned_gains, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"Saved: tuned_gains.json\")\n\n    # Step 4: Run closed-loop control\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"STEP 4: Closed-Loop Control\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    setpoint = sim.get_setpoint()\n\n    control_log = run_closed_loop_control(\n        sim,\n        tuned_gains,\n        setpoint,\n        duration=150.0\n    )\n\n    # Save control log\n    with open(\"/root/control_log.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"Saved: control_log.json\")\n\n    # Step 5: Calculate metrics\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"STEP 5: Performance Metrics\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    metrics = calculate_metrics(control_log)\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open(\"/root/metrics.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"Saved: metrics.json\")\n\n    # Final summary\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"Estimated K = {estimated_params['K']:.4f}, tau = {estimated_params['tau']:.2f}s\")\n    print(f\"Controller gains: Kp={tuned_gains['Kp']:.4f}, Ki={tuned_gains['Ki']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"\\nPerformance vs Requirements:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {metrics['steady_state_error']:.4f}C (target: <0.5C)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {metrics['settling_time']:.2f}s (target: <120s)\" if metrics['settling_time'] else \"  Settling time: N/A\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot: {metrics['overshoot']*100:.2f}% (target: <10%)\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature: {metrics['max_temp']:.2f}C (target: <30C)\")\n    print(f\"  Control duration: 150s (target: >=150s)\")\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"All tasks completed successfully!\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":410,"oldLines":7,"newStart":410,"newLines":7,"lines":["     print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)","     print(\"STEP 3: Controller Gain Tuning\")","     print(\"=\" * 60)","-    tuned_gains = calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=1.5)","+    tuned_gains = calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=1.0)"," ","     # Calibration test to characterize room dynamics\n2. Parameter estimation from calibration data\n3. PID controller gain tuning\n4. Closed-loop control to reach setpoint\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import curve_fit\nfrom hvac_simulator import HVACSimulator\n\n\nclass PIDController:\n    \"\"\"PID controller with anti-windup.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, Kp, Ki, Kd, dt, min_output=0.0, max_output=100.0):\n        self.Kp = Kp\n        self.Ki = Ki\n        self.Kd = Kd\n        self.dt = dt\n        self.min_output = min_output\n        self.max_output = max_output\n\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n    def compute(self, setpoint, measurement):\n        \"\"\"Compute PID output with anti-windup.\"\"\"\n        error = setpoint - measurement\n\n        # Proportional term\n        P = self.Kp * error\n\n        # Integral term (will apply anti-windup below)\n        self.integral += error * self.dt\n        I = self.Ki * self.integral\n\n        # Derivative term\n        derivative = (error - self.prev_error) / self.dt\n        D = self.Kd * derivative\n\n        self.prev_error = error\n\n        # Compute unclamped output\n        output = P + I + D\n\n        # Apply output limits\n        clamped_output = np.clip(output, self.min_output, self.max_output)\n\n        # Anti-windup: back-calculate integral\n        if self.Ki != 0 and output != clamped_output:\n            self.integral -= (output - clamped_output) / self.Ki\n\n        return clamped_output\n\n    def reset(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset controller state.\"\"\"\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n\ndef run_calibration(sim, heater_power=50.0, duration=30.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run open-loop calibration test.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        heater_power: Constant heater power for test (%)\n        duration: Test duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with calibration log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running calibration test: {heater_power}% power for {duration}s\")\n\n    # Extract time and temperature arrays (skip first point where heater is off)\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data[1:]])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data[1:]])\n\n    # Adjust time to start from 0\n    times = times - times[0]\n\n    # Initial temperature (before step)\n    T0 = data[0][\"temperature\"]\n\n    # Define first-order step response model\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        \"\"\"First-order step response: T(t) = T0 + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\"\"\"\n        return T0 + K * heater_power * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Calculate fit quality\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot)\n\n        # Fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.4f}, tau = {tau_est:.2f}s\")\n        print(f\"R² = {r_squared:.4f}, RMSE = {fitting_error:.4f}\")\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        return estimated_params\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        raise\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=1.5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PID controller gains using lambda tuning.\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: Tuning parameter (multiplier of tau)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Calculating controller gains...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # Lambda tuning (IMC-based)\n    lambda_val = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda_val)\n    Ki = 1.0 / lambda_val\n    Kd = 0.0  # Not needed for thermal systems\n\n    print(f\"Controller gains: Kp={Kp:.4f}, Ki={Ki:.4f}, Kd={Kd:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda = {lambda_val:.2f}s\")\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_val, 4)\n    }\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint, duration=150.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # 1. Steady-state error (average of last 20% of data)\n    last_n = max(20, len(temps) // 5)\n    final_temps = temps[-last_n:]\n    avg_final = np.mean(final_temps)\n    steady_state_error = abs(setpoint - avg_final)\n\n    # 5. Maximum temperature\n    max_temp_reached = np.max(temps)\n\n    print(f\"  Rise time: {rise_time:.2f}s\" if rise_time else \"  Rise time: N/A\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {settling_time:.2f}s\" if settling_time else \"  Settling time: N/A\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f}C\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature: {max_temp_reached:.2f}C\")\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4) if rise_time is not None else None,\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4) if settling_time is not None else None,\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp_reached, 4)\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Temperature Controller Implementation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Not needed for thermal systems\n\n    print(f\"Controller gains: Kp={Kp:.4f}, Ki={Ki:.4f}, Kd={Kd:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda = {lambda_val:.2f}s\")\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_val, 4)\n    }\n\n    return tuned_gains","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nTemperature Controller Implementation\n\nThis script performs:\n1. Calibration test to characterize room dynamics\n2. Parameter estimation from calibration data\n3. PID controller gain tuning\n4. Closed-loop control to reach setpoint\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import curve_fit\nfrom hvac_simulator import HVACSimulator\n\n\nclass PIDController:\n    \"\"\"PID controller with anti-windup.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, Kp, Ki, Kd, dt, min_output=0.0, max_output=100.0):\n        self.Kp = Kp\n        self.Ki = Ki\n        self.Kd = Kd\n        self.dt = dt\n        self.min_output = min_output\n        self.max_output = max_output\n\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n    def compute(self, setpoint, measurement):\n        \"\"\"Compute PID output with anti-windup.\"\"\"\n        error = setpoint - measurement\n\n        # Proportional term\n        P = self.Kp * error\n\n        # Integral term (will apply anti-windup below)\n        self.integral += error * self.dt\n        I = self.Ki * self.integral\n\n        # Derivative term\n        derivative = (error - self.prev_error) / self.dt\n        D = self.Kd * derivative\n\n        self.prev_error = error\n\n        # Compute unclamped output\n        output = P + I + D\n\n        # Apply output limits\n        clamped_output = np.clip(output, self.min_output, self.max_output)\n\n        # Anti-windup: back-calculate integral\n        if self.Ki != 0 and output != clamped_output:\n            self.integral -= (output - clamped_output) / self.Ki\n\n        return clamped_output\n\n    def reset(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset controller state.\"\"\"\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n\ndef run_calibration(sim, heater_power=50.0, duration=30.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run open-loop calibration test.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        heater_power: Constant heater power for test (%)\n        duration: Test duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with calibration log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running calibration test: {heater_power}% power for {duration}s\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    initial_temp = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial temperature: {initial_temp:.2f}C\")\n\n    # Collect data with heater off first (initial condition)\n    data = []\n    data.append({\n        \"time\": 0.0,\n        \"temperature\": initial_temp,\n        \"heater_power\": 0.0\n    })\n\n    # Run open-loop test\n    results = sim.run_open_loop(heater_power, duration)\n\n    # Add results to data\n    for result in results:\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": result[\"time\"],\n            \"temperature\": result[\"temperature\"],\n            \"heater_power\": result[\"heater_power\"]\n        })\n\n    print(f\"Calibration complete: {len(data)} data points collected\")\n\n    calibration_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"calibration\",\n        \"heater_power_test\": heater_power,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    return calibration_log\n\n\ndef estimate_parameters(calibration_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Estimate system parameters from calibration data.\n\n    Args:\n        calibration_log: Calibration log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with estimated parameters\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Estimating system parameters from calibration data...\")\n\n    data = calibration_log[\"data\"]\n    heater_power = calibration_log[\"heater_power_test\"]\n\n    # Extract time and temperature arrays (skip first point where heater is off)\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data[1:]])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data[1:]])\n\n    # Adjust time to start from 0\n    times = times - times[0]\n\n    # Initial temperature (before step)\n    T0 = data[0][\"temperature\"]\n\n    # Define first-order step response model\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        \"\"\"First-order step response: T(t) = T0 + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\"\"\"\n        return T0 + K * heater_power * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Initial parameter guesses\n    K_guess = 0.1  # Process gain\n    tau_guess = 30.0  # Time constant\n\n    try:\n        # Fit the model\n        params, covariance = curve_fit(\n            first_order_response,\n            times,\n            temps,\n            p0=[K_guess, tau_guess],\n            bounds=([0.01, 5.0], [0.5, 100.0]),\n            maxfev=5000\n        )\n\n        K_est, tau_est = params\n\n        # Calculate fit quality\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot)\n\n        # Fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.4f}, tau = {tau_est:.2f}s\")\n        print(f\"R² = {r_squared:.4f}, RMSE = {fitting_error:.4f}\")\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        return estimated_params\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        raise\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=1.5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PID controller gains using lambda tuning.\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: Tuning parameter (multiplier of tau)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Calculating controller gains...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # Lambda tuning (IMC-based)\n    lambda_val = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda_val)\n    Ki = 1.0 / lambda_val\n    Kd = 0.0  # Not needed for thermal systems\n\n    print(f\"Controller gains: Kp={Kp:.4f}, Ki={Ki:.4f}, Kd={Kd:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda = {lambda_val:.2f}s\")\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_val, 4)\n    }\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint, duration=150.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Collect control data\n    data = []\n    steps = int(duration / dt)\n\n    # Get initial measurement\n    current_temp = initial_temp\n\n    for i in range(steps):\n        # Compute control output\n        error = setpoint - current_temp\n        heater_power = controller.compute(setpoint, current_temp)\n\n        # Apply control and step simulator\n        result = sim.step(heater_power)\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = result[\"time\"]\n\n        # Log data\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(heater_power, 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })\n\n        # Progress update\n        if (i + 1) % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={current_time:.1f}s: T={current_temp:.2f}C, P={heater_power:.1f}%, e={error:.2f}C\")\n\n    print(f\"Closed-loop control complete: {len(data)} data points\")\n\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    return control_log\n\n\ndef calculate_metrics(control_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate performance metrics from control log.\n\n    Args:\n        control_log: Control log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating performance metrics...\")\n\n    data = control_log[\"data\"]\n    setpoint = control_log[\"setpoint\"]\n\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n    errors = np.array([d[\"error\"] for d in data])\n\n    initial_temp = temps[0]\n    initial_time = times[0]\n\n    # 1. Rise time (time to reach 90% of setpoint from initial)\n    target_90 = initial_temp + 0.9 * (setpoint - initial_temp)\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= target_90)[0]\n    if len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - initial_time\n    else:\n        rise_time = None\n\n    # 2. Overshoot (percentage above setpoint)\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / setpoint\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # 3. Settling time (time to stay within ±2% of setpoint)\n    tolerance = 0.02\n    tolerance_value = abs(setpoint * tolerance)\n    lower_bound = setpoint - tolerance_value\n    upper_bound = setpoint + tolerance_value\n\n    in_band = (temps >= lower_bound) & (temps <= upper_bound)\n\n    # Find last time it was outside the band\n    outside_indices = np.where(~in_band)[0]\n    if len(outside_indices) > 0:\n        settling_idx = outside_indices[-1] + 1\n        if settling_idx < len(times):\n            settling_time = times[settling_idx] - initial_time\n        else:\n            settling_time = None\n    else:\n        # Always in band\n        settling_time = 0.0\n\n    # 4. Steady-state error (average of last 20% of data)\n    last_n = max(20, len(temps) // 5)\n    final_temps = temps[-last_n:]\n    avg_final = np.mean(final_temps)\n    steady_state_error = abs(setpoint - avg_final)\n\n    # 5. Maximum temperature\n    max_temp_reached = np.max(temps)\n\n    print(f\"  Rise time: {rise_time:.2f}s\" if rise_time else \"  Rise time: N/A\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {settling_time:.2f}s\" if settling_time else \"  Settling time: N/A\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f}C\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature: {max_temp_reached:.2f}C\")\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4) if rise_time is not None else None,\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4) if settling_time is not None else None,\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp_reached, 4)\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Temperature Controller Implementation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Calibration test to characterize room dynamics\n2. Parameter estimation from calibration data\n3. PID controller gain tuning\n4. Closed-loop control to reach setpoint\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import curve_fit\nfrom hvac_simulator import HVACSimulator\n\n\nclass PIDController:\n    \"\"\"PID controller with anti-windup.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, Kp, Ki, Kd, dt, min_output=0.0, max_output=100.0):\n        self.Kp = Kp\n        self.Ki = Ki\n        self.Kd = Kd\n        self.dt = dt\n        self.min_output = min_output\n        self.max_output = max_output\n\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n    def compute(self, setpoint, measurement):\n        \"\"\"Compute PID output with anti-windup.\"\"\"\n        error = setpoint - measurement\n\n        # Proportional term\n        P = self.Kp * error\n\n        # Integral term (will apply anti-windup below)\n        self.integral += error * self.dt\n        I = self.Ki * self.integral\n\n        # Derivative term\n        derivative = (error - self.prev_error) / self.dt\n        D = self.Kd * derivative\n\n        self.prev_error = error\n\n        # Compute unclamped output\n        output = P + I + D\n\n        # Apply output limits\n        clamped_output = np.clip(output, self.min_output, self.max_output)\n\n        # Anti-windup: back-calculate integral\n        if self.Ki != 0 and output != clamped_output:\n            self.integral -= (output - clamped_output) / self.Ki\n\n        return clamped_output\n\n    def reset(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset controller state.\"\"\"\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n\ndef run_calibration(sim, heater_power=50.0, duration=30.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run open-loop calibration test.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        heater_power: Constant heater power for test (%)\n        duration: Test duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with calibration log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running calibration test: {heater_power}% power for {duration}s\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    initial_temp = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial temperature: {initial_temp:.2f}C\")\n\n    # Collect data with heater off first (initial condition)\n    data = []\n    data.append({\n        \"time\": 0.0,\n        \"temperature\": initial_temp,\n        \"heater_power\": 0.0\n    })\n\n    # Run open-loop test\n    results = sim.run_open_loop(heater_power, duration)\n\n    # Add results to data\n    for result in results:\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": result[\"time\"],\n            \"temperature\": result[\"temperature\"],\n            \"heater_power\": result[\"heater_power\"]\n        })\n\n    print(f\"Calibration complete: {len(data)} data points collected\")\n\n    calibration_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"calibration\",\n        \"heater_power_test\": heater_power,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    return calibration_log\n\n\ndef estimate_parameters(calibration_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Estimate system parameters from calibration data.\n\n    Args:\n        calibration_log: Calibration log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with estimated parameters\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Estimating system parameters from calibration data...\")\n\n    data = calibration_log[\"data\"]\n    heater_power = calibration_log[\"heater_power_test\"]\n\n    # Extract time and temperature arrays (skip first point where heater is off)\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data[1:]])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data[1:]])\n\n    # Adjust time to start from 0\n    times = times - times[0]\n\n    # Initial temperature (before step)\n    T0 = data[0][\"temperature\"]\n\n    # Define first-order step response model\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        \"\"\"First-order step response: T(t) = T0 + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\"\"\"\n        return T0 + K * heater_power * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Initial parameter guesses\n    K_guess = 0.1  # Process gain\n    tau_guess = 30.0  # Time constant\n\n    try:\n        # Fit the model\n        params, covariance = curve_fit(\n            first_order_response,\n            times,\n            temps,\n            p0=[K_guess, tau_guess],\n            bounds=([0.01, 5.0], [0.5, 100.0]),\n            maxfev=5000\n        )\n\n        K_est, tau_est = params\n\n        # Calculate fit quality\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot)\n\n        # Fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.4f}, tau = {tau_est:.2f}s\")\n        print(f\"R² = {r_squared:.4f}, RMSE = {fitting_error:.4f}\")\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        return estimated_params\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        raise\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=1.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PID controller gains using modified lambda tuning.\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: Tuning parameter (multiplier of tau, smaller = more aggressive)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Calculating controller gains...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # Modified lambda tuning (IMC-based) with aggressive integral\n    lambda_val = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda_val)\n    Ki = 1.0 / lambda_val\n\n    # Boost Ki for faster settling (multiply by 3 for thermal systems)\n    Ki = Ki * 3.0\n\n    Kd = 0.0  # Not needed for thermal systems\n\n    print(f\"Controller gains: Kp={Kp:.4f}, Ki={Ki:.4f}, Kd={Kd:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda = {lambda_val:.2f}s\")\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_val, 4)\n    }\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint, duration=150.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Reset simulator to initial conditions\n    initial_temp = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Reset to initial temperature: {initial_temp:.2f}C\")\n\n    # Initialize PID controller\n    dt = sim.get_dt()\n    controller = PIDController(\n        Kp=tuned_gains[\"Kp\"],\n        Ki=tuned_gains[\"Ki\"],\n        Kd=tuned_gains[\"Kd\"],\n        dt=dt,\n        min_output=0.0,\n        max_output=100.0\n    )\n\n    # Collect control data\n    data = []\n    steps = int(duration / dt)\n\n    # Get initial measurement\n    current_temp = initial_temp\n\n    for i in range(steps):\n        # Compute control output\n        error = setpoint - current_temp\n        heater_power = controller.compute(setpoint, current_temp)\n\n        # Apply control and step simulator\n        result = sim.step(heater_power)\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = result[\"time\"]\n\n        # Log data\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(heater_power, 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })\n\n        # Progress update\n        if (i + 1) % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={current_time:.1f}s: T={current_temp:.2f}C, P={heater_power:.1f}%, e={error:.2f}C\")\n\n    print(f\"Closed-loop control complete: {len(data)} data points\")\n\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    return control_log\n\n\ndef calculate_metrics(control_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate performance metrics from control log.\n\n    Args:\n        control_log: Control log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating performance metrics...\")\n\n    data = control_log[\"data\"]\n    setpoint = control_log[\"setpoint\"]\n\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n    errors = np.array([d[\"error\"] for d in data])\n\n    initial_temp = temps[0]\n    initial_time = times[0]\n\n    # 1. Rise time (time to reach 90% of setpoint from initial)\n    target_90 = initial_temp + 0.9 * (setpoint - initial_temp)\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= target_90)[0]\n    if len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - initial_time\n    else:\n        rise_time = None\n\n    # 2. Overshoot (percentage above setpoint)\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / setpoint\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # 3. Settling time (time to stay within ±2% of setpoint)\n    tolerance = 0.02\n    tolerance_value = abs(setpoint * tolerance)\n    lower_bound = setpoint - tolerance_value\n    upper_bound = setpoint + tolerance_value\n\n    in_band = (temps >= lower_bound) & (temps <= upper_bound)\n\n    # Find last time it was outside the band\n    outside_indices = np.where(~in_band)[0]\n    if len(outside_indices) > 0:\n        settling_idx = outside_indices[-1] + 1\n        if settling_idx < len(times):\n            settling_time = times[settling_idx] - initial_time\n        else:\n            settling_time = None\n    else:\n        # Always in band\n        settling_time = 0.0\n\n    # 4. Steady-state error (average of last 20% of data)\n    last_n = max(20, len(temps) // 5)\n    final_temps = temps[-last_n:]\n    avg_final = np.mean(final_temps)\n    steady_state_error = abs(setpoint - avg_final)\n\n    # 5. Maximum temperature\n    max_temp_reached = np.max(temps)\n\n    print(f\"  Rise time: {rise_time:.2f}s\" if rise_time else \"  Rise time: N/A\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {settling_time:.2f}s\" if settling_time else \"  Settling time: N/A\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f}C\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature: {max_temp_reached:.2f}C\")\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4) if rise_time is not None else None,\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4) if settling_time is not None else None,\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp_reached, 4)\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Temperature Controller Implementation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Save calibration log\n    with open(\"/root/calibration_log.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(calibration_log, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"Saved: calibration_log.json\")\n\n    # Step 2: Estimate parameters\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"STEP 2: Parameter Estimation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    estimated_params = estimate_parameters(calibration_log)\n\n    # Save estimated parameters\n    with open(\"/root/estimated_params.json\", \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(estimated_params, f, indent=2)\n    print(\"Saved: estimated_params.json\")\n\n    # Step 3: Calculate controller gains\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"STEP 3: Controller Gain Tuning\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    tuned_gains = calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=0.8)\n\n    # Calibration test to characterize room dynamics\n2. Parameter estimation from calibration data\n3. PID controller gain tuning\n4. Closed-loop control to reach setpoint\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import curve_fit\nfrom hvac_simulator import HVACSimulator\n\n\nclass PIDController:\n    \"\"\"PID controller with anti-windup.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, Kp, Ki, Kd, dt, min_output=0.0, max_output=100.0):\n        self.Kp = Kp\n        self.Ki = Ki\n        self.Kd = Kd\n        self.dt = dt\n        self.min_output = min_output\n        self.max_output = max_output\n\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n    def compute(self, setpoint, measurement):\n        \"\"\"Compute PID output with anti-windup.\"\"\"\n        error = setpoint - measurement\n\n        # Proportional term\n        P = self.Kp * error\n\n        # Integral term (will apply anti-windup below)\n        self.integral += error * self.dt\n        I = self.Ki * self.integral\n\n        # Derivative term\n        derivative = (error - self.prev_error) / self.dt\n        D = self.Kd * derivative\n\n        self.prev_error = error\n\n        # Compute unclamped output\n        output = P + I + D\n\n        # Apply output limits\n        clamped_output = np.clip(output, self.min_output, self.max_output)\n\n        # Anti-windup: back-calculate integral\n        if self.Ki != 0 and output != clamped_output:\n            self.integral -= (output - clamped_output) / self.Ki\n\n        return clamped_output\n\n    def reset(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset controller state.\"\"\"\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n\ndef run_calibration(sim, heater_power=50.0, duration=30.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run open-loop calibration test.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        heater_power: Constant heater power for test (%)\n        duration: Test duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with calibration log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running calibration test: {heater_power}% power for {duration}s\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    initial_temp = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial temperature: {initial_temp:.2f}C\")\n\n    # Collect data with heater off first (initial condition)\n    data = []\n    data.append({\n        \"time\": 0.0,\n        \"temperature\": initial_temp,\n        \"heater_power\": 0.0\n    })\n\n    # Run open-loop test\n    results = sim.run_open_loop(heater_power, duration)\n\n    # Add results to data\n    for result in results:\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": result[\"time\"],\n            \"temperature\": result[\"temperature\"],\n            \"heater_power\": result[\"heater_power\"]\n        })\n\n    print(f\"Calibration complete: {len(data)} data points collected\")\n\n    calibration_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"calibration\",\n        \"heater_power_test\": heater_power,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    return calibration_log\n\n\ndef estimate_parameters(calibration_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Estimate system parameters from calibration data.\n\n    Args:\n        calibration_log: Calibration log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with estimated parameters\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Estimating system parameters from calibration data...\")\n\n    data = calibration_log[\"data\"]\n    heater_power = calibration_log[\"heater_power_test\"]\n\n    # Extract time and temperature arrays (skip first point where heater is off)\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data[1:]])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data[1:]])\n\n    # Adjust time to start from 0\n    times = times - times[0]\n\n    # Initial temperature (before step)\n    T0 = data[0][\"temperature\"]\n\n    # Define first-order step response model\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        \"\"\"First-order step response: T(t) = T0 + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\"\"\"\n        return T0 + K * heater_power * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Initial parameter guesses\n    K_guess = 0.1  # Process gain\n    tau_guess = 30.0  # Time constant\n\n    try:\n        # Fit the model\n        params, covariance = curve_fit(\n            first_order_response,\n            times,\n            temps,\n            p0=[K_guess, tau_guess],\n            bounds=([0.01, 5.0], [0.5, 100.0]),\n            maxfev=5000\n        )\n\n        K_est, tau_est = params\n\n        # Calculate fit quality\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot)\n\n        # Fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.4f}, tau = {tau_est:.2f}s\")\n        print(f\"R² = {r_squared:.4f}, RMSE = {fitting_error:.4f}\")\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        return estimated_params\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        raise\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=1.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PID controller gains using modified lambda tuning.\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: Tuning parameter (multiplier of tau, smaller = more aggressive)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Calculating controller gains...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # Modified lambda tuning (IMC-based) with aggressive integral\n    lambda_val = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda_val)\n    Ki = 1.0 / lambda_val\n\n    # Boost Ki for faster settling (multiply by 5 for thermal systems)\n    Ki = Ki * 5.0\n\n    Kd = 0.0  # Not needed for thermal systems\n\n    print(f\"Controller gains: Kp={Kp:.4f}, Ki={Ki:.4f}, Kd={Kd:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda = {lambda_val:.2f}s\")\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_val, 4)\n    }\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint, duration=150.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Collect control data\n    data = []\n    steps = int(duration / dt)\n\n    # Get initial measurement\n    current_temp = initial_temp\n\n    for i in range(steps):\n        # Compute control output\n        error = setpoint - current_temp\n        heater_power = controller.compute(setpoint, current_temp)\n\n        # Apply control and step simulator\n        result = sim.step(heater_power)\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = result[\"time\"]\n\n        # Log data\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(heater_power, 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })\n\n        # Progress update\n        if (i + 1) % 100 == 0:\n            print(f\"  t={current_time:.1f}s: T={current_temp:.2f}C, P={heater_power:.1f}%, e={error:.2f}C\")\n\n    print(f\"Closed-loop control complete: {len(data)} data points\")\n\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    return control_log\n\n\ndef calculate_metrics(control_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate performance metrics from control log.\n\n    Args:\n        control_log: Control log dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating performance metrics...\")\n\n    data = control_log[\"data\"]\n    setpoint = control_log[\"setpoint\"]\n\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n    errors = np.array([d[\"error\"] for d in data])\n\n    initial_temp = temps[0]\n    initial_time = times[0]\n\n    # 1. Rise time (time to reach 90% of setpoint from initial)\n    target_90 = initial_temp + 0.9 * (setpoint - initial_temp)\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= target_90)[0]\n    if len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - initial_time\n    else:\n        rise_time = None\n\n    # 2. Overshoot (percentage above setpoint)\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / setpoint\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # 3. Settling time (time to stay within ±2% of setpoint)\n    tolerance = 0.02\n    tolerance_value = abs(setpoint * tolerance)\n    lower_bound = setpoint - tolerance_value\n    upper_bound = setpoint + tolerance_value\n\n    in_band = (temps >= lower_bound) & (temps <= upper_bound)\n\n    # Find last time it was outside the band\n    outside_indices = np.where(~in_band)[0]\n    if len(outside_indices) > 0:\n        settling_idx = outside_indices[-1] + 1\n        if settling_idx < len(times):\n            settling_time = times[settling_idx] - initial_time\n        else:\n            settling_time = None\n    else:\n        # Always in band\n        settling_time = 0.0\n\n    # 4. Steady-state error (average of last 20% of data)\n    last_n = max(20, len(temps) // 5)\n    final_temps = temps[-last_n:]\n    avg_final = np.mean(final_temps)\n    steady_state_error = abs(setpoint - avg_final)\n\n    # 5. Maximum temperature\n    max_temp_reached = np.max(temps)\n\n    print(f\"  Rise time: {rise_time:.2f}s\" if rise_time else \"  Rise time: N/A\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time: {settling_time:.2f}s\" if settling_time else \"  Settling time: N/A\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f}C\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature: {max_temp_reached:.2f}C\")\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4) if rise_time is not None else None,\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4) if settling_time is not None else None,\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp_reached, 4)\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Temperature Controller Implementation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # The solution includes system identification, controller tuning, and closed-loop control.\n\n## Project Structure\n\n```\n/root/\n├── temperature_controller.py    # Main implementation\n├── hvac_simulator.py            # HVAC simulator (provided)\n├── room_config.json             # Configuration (provided)\n├── calibration_log.json         # Calibration test results\n├── estimated_params.json        # Estimated system parameters\n├── tuned_gains.json            # PID controller gains\n├── control_log.json            # Control loop data\n├── metrics.json                # Performance metrics\n└── environment/skills/         # Skill documents\n    ├── first-order-system-identification.md\n    ├── pid-controller-tuning.md\n    ├── control-system-metrics.md\n    ├── scipy-curve-fitting.md\n    └── numpy-data-processing.md\n```\n\n## Implementation Steps\n\n### 1. Calibration Test\n- Applied 50% heater power for 30 seconds\n- Collected 61 data points (0.5s sampling rate)\n- Output: `calibration_log.json`\n\n### 2. Parameter Estimation\n- Used scipy.optimize.curve_fit to fit first-order model\n- Estimated process gain (K) and time constant (τ)\n- Achieved R² = 0.9866 (excellent fit)\n- Output: `estimated_params.json`\n\n**Results:**\n- K = 0.1022 (°C per % heater power)\n- τ = 30.21 seconds\n\n### 3. Controller Gain Tuning\n- Used modified lambda tuning method (IMC-based)\n- Lambda factor = 0.8τ for aggressive response\n- Boosted integral gain by 6× for thermal systems\n- Output: `tuned_gains.json`\n\n**Results:**\n- Kp = 12.23\n- Ki = 0.248\n- Kd = 0.0\n\n### 4. Closed-Loop Control\n- Reset simulator to initial conditions (~18°C)\n- Ran PID controller for 150 seconds\n- Implemented anti-windup to handle power limits (0-100%)\n- Output: `control_log.json`\n\n### 5. Performance Metrics\n- Calculated rise time, overshoot, settling time, steady-state error\n- All requirements met!\n- Output: `metrics.json`\n\n## Requirements vs Results\n\n| Requirement | Target | Achieved | Status |\n|------------|--------|----------|--------|\n| Steady-state error | < 0.5°C | 0.128°C | ✓ PASS |\n| Settling time | < 120s | 118.5s | ✓ PASS |\n| Overshoot | < 10% | 0.85% | ✓ PASS |\n| Max temperature | < 30°C | 22.19°C | ✓ PASS |\n| Control duration | ≥ 150s | 150s | ✓ PASS |\n| Calibration points | ≥ 20 | 61 | ✓ PASS |\n\n## Key Technical Decisions\n\n1. **First-Order Model**: The HVAC system follows first-order dynamics, making it ideal for step response identification.\n\n2. **Lambda Tuning**: IMC-based tuning provides systematic controller design with guaranteed stability.\n\n3. **Integral Boost**: Standard lambda tuning is conservative; we boosted Ki by 6× to meet aggressive settling time requirements while maintaining stability.\n\n4. **Anti-Windup**: Critical for systems with actuator limits (0-100% heater power) to prevent integral windup.\n\n5. **No Derivative Action**: Thermal systems typically don't benefit from derivative action due to measurement noise and slow dynamics.\n\n## Usage\n\n```bash\n# Run the complete implementation\npython3 temperature_controller.py\n\n# This will generate all required JSON files\n```\n\n## Skill Documents\n\nFive reusable skill documents were created in `/root/environment/skills/`:\n\n1. **first-order-system-identification.md** - Using curve_fit for parameter estimation\n5. **numpy-data-processing.md** - Array operations for control systems\n\n## Dependencies\n\n- Python 3\n- NumPy (array operations, mathematical functions)\n- SciPy (curve fitting for parameter estimation)\n\n## Implementation Highlights\n\n- **Robust Parameter Estimation**: Bounds and initial guesses ensure curve_fit converges\n- **Real-time Progress**: Console output shows controller progress every 50 seconds\n- **Clean Architecture**: Separate functions for each task (calibration, estimation, tuning, control, metrics)\n- **Error Handling**: Try-except blocks for curve fitting failures\n- **Anti-Windup**: Prevents integral windup when heater saturates\n\n## Mathematical Models\n\n**First-Order Dynamics:**\n```\ndT/dt = (1/τ) * (K * u + T_ambient - T)\n```\n\n**PID Controller:**\n```\nu(t) = Kp * e(t) + Ki * ∫e(τ)dτ + Kd * de(t)/dt\n```\n\n**Lambda Tuning:**\n```\nKp = τ / (K * λ)\nKi = 1 / λ\nλ = 0.8 * τ (with 6× boost on Ki)\n```\n\n## Results\n\nThe controller successfully:\n- Maintains temperature at 22.0°C ± 0.13°C\n- Settles in 118.5 seconds (1.5% faster than requirement)\n- Minimal overshoot (0.85%)\n- Never exceeds safety limits\n- Smooth, stable control throughout 150s duration\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":290,"cache_read_input_tokens":53865,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":290,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b027f611-89d7-4d0d-b364-9bf88c4dfb87","uuid":"26e7be99-c23d-4e73-8b6d-28e8f1408ec9"}
